# Transmission

Journal: `quests/main_quest/act_01/q110_03_cyberspace` · `MainQuest` · hash `495629330`.

Journal premise: It actually fucking worked. Working with the Voodoo Boys, Brigitte… It leads us straight to Alt. Looks like your troubles are over, kid. Fixing your head is gonna be a walk in the park for Alt, that I guarantee. You just do what Brigitte says for now – I'll handle the rest.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 84 exact journal-linked nodes in 18 resources; 35 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 32 content candidate, 3 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

Root quest resources with additional journal references: none indexed. Roots remain activation context when a phase/scene anchor exists, to avoid importing sibling launch scaffolding.

## Choice labels, eligibility and outgoing branches

Text is joined from screenplay option IDs to the embedded localization store. An option-level predicate is only one layer of eligibility; parent graph routes can impose additional gates.

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

2064 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 299 distinct direct resource identities; 76 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
